    &quot;Database&quot; : { &quot;Firebase&quot;, &quot;Sqlite&quot;, &quot;PostgreSql&quot; },
    &quot;Editors&quot; : { &quot;Vscode&quot;, &quot;Xcode&quot;, &quot;Sublime&quot;, &quot;Neovim&quot; },
    &quot;Platforms&quot; : { &quot;Mac&quot;, &quot;GNU/Linux&quot;, &quot;Windows&quot; },
    &quot;OtherTools&quot; : { &quot;Git&quot;, &quot;Figma&quot;, &quot;Photoshop&quot;, &quot;Gimp&quot;, &quot;Lightroom&quot; }
  };
}"><pre><span class="pl-c">// tools_I_use organized</span>
